Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any parabolic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 502. The projective resolution has length at most 417 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on parabolic structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 502. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 417. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The parabolic operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 769. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 545 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to parabolic theory, we decompose the problem into 769 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 545. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
